Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The discrete_math variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 691 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 371 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by discrete_math generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 371. QED.
